- [x] 2026-08-13 | c6c6c04 | flash_cards.html | **The vowel group headers stop saying the colour in English** — `authored-but-unreferenced i18n key family` (S179 Pass A, part d), and **the flash-cards variety debt the S199 pointer called urgent: this tool had gone SEVEN consecutive sessions untouched** (last S192). The `flashcards.vowelgroup.header` template was wired and translated, but its `{color}` param was fed the raw English `g.colorHtml`, so a Hebrew UI read **"צליל AH – Aqua"** for all seven groups — half translated, on the setup screen a teacher sees first. The nine `flashcards.vowelgroup.color_*` keys already existed, fully translated, referenced nowhere. **The interesting half is why this was not a one-line key swap:** the English label carries a teaching mnemonic (`<u>A</u>qua` = "A for AH"), so naively switching to the key would have silently dropped the underline from every English header — a regression traded for a fix. **The generator had already solved exactly this** (`vowelColorLabel`: use the translation only when it actually differs from the English word), so the fix is a convergence, not an invention; `colorName` is always one word, so lowercasing IS the whole slugify its `i18nName` would do. `color_navy`/`color_orange` stay unreferenced **on purpose** — they belong to the TaL AM scheme, which hides group headers entirely. Verified he/en × light/dark × 1280/800: 7/7 headers Hebrew in HE, and the **control arm confirms all 7 English headers keep their `<u>` in every cell**; a live EN→HE→EN switch re-renders through `applyI18n` (which already called `initVowels`) and restores `<u>` 7/7. 0 pageerrors in all 8 cells.

- [x] 2026-08-12 | b5bd9c0 | torah_trainer.html | **The "data files failed to load" message stops being overwritten a moment later** — Pass B's one finding, and a **NEW registered pattern** (`error-status-clobbered-by-a-later-routine-write`). When init's fetch of `parshiyot.json` / `pockettorah/*.json` fails — a school network blocking the origin, a partial deploy, a stale cache — the catch sets a diagnostic naming the missing files. init then continues to `fetchAndRender()`, which resolves no reading and, in its `if (!r)` branch, **overwrote that line with "Pick a parsha to start."** So the only message explaining the failure was destroyed inside the same init, and what remained instructed the teacher to pick from a list the failure had just emptied: the picker holds only the 17 local `HOLIDAY_READINGS`, with all 54 parshiyot gone. **Detected by reading `#ttStatus` under a blocked corpus, not by reading the code** — the code looks correct at both sites in isolation. Fix uses existing state (`!parshiyotData.length` is reachable only on that failure) and **two keys that already existed**, so no new copy and no CSV change; the catch also now passes its key to `setStatus` so a language switch re-resolves the line. Verified: blocked corpus → diagnostic holds in EN **and** HE with error styling and the key set; **CONTROL with the corpus present and no reading forced → still "Pick a parsha to start."**, so the normal path is untouched; language switch re-localizes the held message; light/dark × 1280/800 all 0 pageerrors.

- [x] 2026-08-12 | 27554b4 | torah_trainer.html + hebrew_dictionary.html | **The tour step counter stops promising steps it will never show** — S197 Pass I's headline and a **NEW registered pattern** (`content-dependent-tour-step-miscounts-the-tour`). A step whose content never arrived resolves to null and is skipped **silently by design**; the counter was built from the raw array index, so it **jumped and over-promised**: torah with no reading `"Step 3 of 6" → "Step 6 of 6"`, dictionary with its corpus blocked `"Step 4 of 7" → "Step 6 of 7"`. Both are real school scenarios — a network that blocks the corpus (the case f72e0f8 already wrote a teacher-facing message for), a Sefaria outage, or simply no parsha picked yet. **Fix: count by PRESENCE of each step's target, never visibility.** Absent means the content isn't there at all; merely-hidden means a collapsed panel that opens fine once the tour reaches it. Presence is also the **only side-effect-free probe available** — testing visibility would mean running every step's `reveal()`, which in the dictionary opens the sidebar and expands panels the tour hasn't reached (the exact artifact this pass's own first probe hit). The step being shown always counts itself, so torah's `_tourHoldForReading` (parked on a step whose target hasn't rendered) still numbers correctly. **torah `1,2,3,6 of 6` → `1,2,3,4 of 4`; dictionary `1,2,3,4,6,7 of 7` → `1..6 of 6`; both unchanged when the content loads.** Verified 12/12 matrix cells (light/dark × 1280/800 × three states): no jump, stable total, walk ends exactly at that total, 0 pageerrors, 0 vacuous runs; the 5 untouched tours unchanged.

The function's own comment says "each move batch" — the call sits **inside** the per-sample loop, so the code was already more expensive than its documented contract. Measured @4× CPU, last-10% mean per sample / frames over the 16.7 ms budget, as stroke length grows to the 2048-pt cap: **round 0.56→1.92 ms (0→4 frames)**, **chisel 1.9→10.98 (0→3)**, **brush 2.4→13.93 (0→28)**, **hollow 2.55→34.35 (0→408)**. The pain does not wait for the cap: **hollow is already over budget on 153 samples at n=1200** — one slow sweeping stroke on a classroom tablet. Fix is the tool's own idiom, used twice already — `boldGenSet` (itself **the S85 Pass D fix for exactly this shape**, reintroduced by draw mode 111 sessions later) and `nodeStatusCoords`: coalesce to one repaint per animation frame, no snapshot, the frame re-reads `g.pts`. The round pen's incremental append is **deliberately left alone** — measured, it never blows the budget. Verified @4×, light/dark × 1280/800, with **paced** input so rAF actually runs (a synchronous dispatch loop starves rAF and would fake a 100× win): `_drawLivePathD` invocations **884 → 76** (one per frame) with the instrumentation wrap **asserting it intercepted** rather than assuming, accepted points **884 unchanged**, **10/10 cells byte-identical** on both committed stroke JSON and rendered path `d`, preview still animating at **75 distinct live states** (same as baseline), wall clock **2.6–3.8× faster** for styled pens with round unchanged as a **1.00× negative control**, a real trusted-mouse stroke (`page.mouse`) committing correctly in both themes, and `pageerror == 0` everywhere.

Memoized the parse on the exact stored string: a different raw string — another tab wrote, or a write failed — misses the cache and re-parses, so a stale profile can never be served. **Every one of the 21 `readProfiles()` call sites was audited for the aliasing hazard this introduces** (a caller mutating the shared object without writing): all of them write immediately, with no early return between mutation and write — `deleteProfile` and `renameProfile` both bail *before* touching the object. `writeProfiles` adopts the object as the cache **only after a successful `setItem`** and **drops the cache on failure**, which preserves today's truthful behaviour on a quota error: the teacher keeps seeing the state that actually reached the disk, not the mutation that was lost. **renderProfiles 408→35 ms (5 students), 2,409→119 ms (8), 10,602→301 ms (20); renderLadderCard 187→0.1 ms, openLadderMap 188→0.7 ms, collectMissedCards 86→0.1 ms.** The residual 301 ms at 20 students is **one** cold parse of a 4.8 MB blob, not 47 of them. (3) **The export shaping gate NEVER ran** — harfbuzzjs 0.4.6's `hb.wasm` declares wasi/env imports and `hbjs.js` needs emscripten's `addFunction`, so `WebAssembly.instantiate(wasm, {})` threw unconditionally and `harfbuzzGate` returned `{skipped:true}` (console.warn only) on every export since it shipped — the v5.4 "verify the וֹ ligature" guard was verifying nothing. - [x] 2026-07-08 | cae2fbb | hebrew_blend_generator.html | **P2 (S32 Pass G headline — "Landscape Print" now actually rotates `window.print()`)** — the toggle set `body.landscape-mode` (which only widened the on-screen blends grid to 10 cols) but was **disconnected from the paper orientation**: `window.print()` is bare, the S31 `@page` set `size:` with no orientation keyword (defaults portrait), and the only landscape print CSS keyed off the *print-dialog* orientation, not the toggle — so toggling Landscape + clicking 🖨 Print still gave a **portrait** page. - [x] 2026-07-27 | 2eb5775 | CLAUDE.md | **Offline/failure tests must open the context with `serviceWorkers: 'block'`** (S165 — the session's most reusable finding). `sw.js` calls `skipWaiting()` + `clients.claim()`, so the worker activates on the **first** load and then serves `/data/` from the version-independent `DATA_CACHE` — and **service-worker requests bypass Playwright's `page.route`**. A route that aborts `/data/` therefore stops blocking the moment the worker is live: the fetch succeeds, no failure path runs, and the test reports the happy path while looking like it tested failure. Added to the Playwright recipe with the one-line fix.

Before writing any code I instrumented every write to `#wordsCount` and found `ALL_WORDS_DATA` **populated** — the block had never held (the service worker was serving the corpus), and the "0 words in pool" text was simply correct for the default `wordsSource === 'mylists'` with no saved lists. Re-tested with `serviceWorkers: 'block'` and `wordsSource = 'alldict'`: Flash Cards shows **"Failed to load dictionary words." / "טעינת מילות המילון נכשלה."** — a proper localized message. **No code changed.** The value of the iteration was catching it before shipping a fix for a non-bug, and the harness rule it produced (2eb5775).

- [x] 2026-07-27 | 9ba18e5 | torah_trainer.html | **The reading area showed developer key names when data was blocked** (S165 Pass F; P2). `torah.reading.empty_title`, `torah.reading.empty_body` and `torah.status.pick_parsha` rendered literally, **in both languages** — the **third instance** of the `pre-ready-i18n / never-re-rendered` pattern (registered S152, hit again S158). - [x] 2026-07-27 | 02f4ed0 | hebrew_dictionary.html | **The Dictionary hung on "Loading dictionary…" forever when the network blocked its corpus** (S165 Pass F; **P2, and the only tool of seven that crashed**). The corpus fetch is kicked off at **parse time** while `js/i18n.js` is **deferred**, so a fast failure lands in `.catch()` before `I18n` exists — and a bare `I18n.t()` on an undeclared global **throws a ReferenceError**, killing the handler before it could set the message or hide the progress bar. The teacher got a permanent spinner and an uncaught error. **FIXED:** rather than only guarding the call, the handler re-points the element's `data-i18n` at the failure key and paints the English fallback immediately — correct under **both** orderings (I18n already up → right language at once; I18n later → `applyStaticI18n()` fills it, and a later language switch re-translates it, which a plain guard would not). Fallback is the CSV's own `en` value, so **no new copy**. **Verified:** data blocked × EN/HE × light/dark, **plus the same matrix with `js/i18n.js` deliberately delayed 1.2 s past the failure** to force the pre-ready ordering — all 8 show the correct localized message, bar hidden, 0 pageerror; happy path unaffected (loader hides, 100 result cards); pre-fix replay reproduces the ReferenceError.

- [x] ✅ 2026-07-28 (pre-merge adversarial audit of the Learner Ladder — maintainer-requested, fixes committed on the same branch) | flash_cards.html + 3 CSV rewords | **The audit's headline: level 8 "Add Shva" was a functional no-op, green tests notwithstanding.** `generateCard` never allows shva as a syllable **nucleus** (Rule N1 — a `'sh'` pick is rerolled then hard-rejected); shva renders ONLY as the automatic *connector* on a non-vowelled position before the last vowel (בְּרָ). (2) **Auto-save banks new-best only** — the first attempt at a level always banks (a first fail is exactly the weakness signal) and retries bank only on an improved `bestPct`, so Try-Again grinding can no longer flood the 50-entry history cap and silently evict the teacher's own saved drills (the audit's second should-fix). Three level descriptions reworded (EN+HE, maintainer-approved): `desc_shva` (the connector pattern), `desc_allvowels` (drops the false "thirteen"), `desc_blend2a` (names its four vowels — the old "first four" contradicted its own key list, which swaps tzere for chirik by design). **Verified: suite upgraded to 214 checks incl. the missing class — occurrence assertions** (every shva-level card carries the connector on letter 1 across ~250 draws; all three hatafs appear in `allvowels`; non-connector levels stay shva-free), new-best-only banking counts, language-switch button state, a My-Fonts byte-identical round trip, word-pool restore, and a forced-throw exit that must strand nothing; report suite still 104/104; negative control still discriminates. Audit also re-confirmed all structural invariants independently (single `SETTINGS_KEY` write site, no chokepoint recursion, no XSS — a missing i18n key *drops* an interpolation rather than injecting it, letter groups exactly partition the 31-letter display set). - [x] ✅ SHIPPED 2026-07-28 (user-directed), 7bff4f2 | flash_cards.html | **Learner Ladder — a guided decoding path.** Every drill in this tool is teacher-configured, so a learner opening the page alone had no answer to *"where do I start?"* and no sense of progress between sessions. A **🪜 Learning Path** card at the top of the setup screen opens a level map of **12 ordered levels** — letter names (3), then one vowel family at a time (6), then two- and three-letter blends (3) — each one tap: configure → drill → score → record → unlock the next at **80%** (deliberately the same threshold as `renderProfileHistory`'s ✓, so "passed" and "✓" never disagree). **No second deck generator and no pedagogy engine** — a level is just a locked-down configuration of the existing machinery, held in one `FC_LADDER` data array so content and mechanics stay separable. **The core of the change is settings safety.** `saveSettings()` has **~70 call sites**, so a level that reconfigures the drill would otherwise persist itself over the teacher's setup; following torah_trainer's `_handoutEnter`/`_handoutExit`, a level snapshots `getSettings()`, applies overrides, and restores on the way out with `saveSettings()` a **no-op for the duration**. **`showScreen()` turned out to be a 3-line chokepoint**, so ONE guard there covers every route home (header button, End Practice Early, Return to Options) instead of a restore sprinkled at each call site; `_ladderEnter` also unwinds if `startPractice` bails, which it can on an empty deck. Levels **pin** the deck-shaping fields and **inherit** presentation (font, colours, audio, translit style), so a snapshot carrying Other mode, a letter lock or listening can't quietly produce the wrong deck. **Blend levels pin `vowelPositions` to every position:** the app default (`{mode}`) puts a **shva connector** on the earlier letters (בְּרָ), and a first blend drill wants plain CV-CV (בָּרָ). **Progress lives inside the existing profile object** as `ladder`, so **no new localStorage key, no AllTools wiring, no migration** — `.ivrit` backup already exports profiles wholesale; **unlock state is derived from `bestPct`, never stored**, so no pointer can de-sync across a merge. **Two latent field-dropping merge sites found and fixed:** `renameProfile` and `mergeProfilesBlob` both rebuild a profile from `results` alone and silently dropped the new field — both now merge it (higher `bestPct` wins, **earliest** `passedAt` kept). **Two re-entrancy hazards handled:** Redo / Practice-Mistakes replay a deck that is no longer the level's own draw, so they **detach the scoring claim while keeping the settings binding** (restoring mid-deck would swap the configuration out from under the open cards); and the record + auto-save are guarded because **`applyI18n` re-runs `showResults` on a language switch**, which must not bank the same drill twice. `saveCurrentResult`'s write step is extracted as **`saveResultToProfile`** so the ladder's auto-save shares it rather than forking storage — ladder drills then feed weakness tracking and the progress report for free. **43** new `flashcards.ladder.*` keys (EN+HE, **gate-2 approved**; one planned key dropped in favour of reusing `results.save_button_done`). **Gate 1 (the 12-level progression + letter/vowel groupings) was also maintainer-approved** — pedagogy is the maintainer's call, not the implementer's. Verified headless (`serviceWorkers:'block'`, external origins aborted): **184 checks, 0 pageerror** — settings **byte-identical** across a full multi-level session, pass/fail/replay-lower paths, both merge sites, locked-row + focus behaviour, no-profile flow, `startPractice` bail-out, XSS probe, EN+HE with RTL **measured**, light/dark × 1280/800. **Deck fidelity is asserted on the rendered nikkud** (~150 draws per level) after the first attempt passed **vacuously** — cards are only `{heb, translit}`, so the intended `vowelKeys`/`letterHebs` reads were `undefined`; the rewrite classifies U+05BC by context (dagesh in בּ vs. **shuruk** in וּ) and is backed by a **negative control** proving it detects injected violations. The progress-report suite still passes **104/104** after the refactor. `sw.js` v424→v425.

- [x] 2026-08-08 | 74e0530 | Hebrew_Font_Maker.html | **The side-bearing band no longer vanishes on a left-overhanging letter** (S187 Pass B — the pass's only finding, and a NEW registered pattern). `letterRSB()` clamps its derived value with `Math.max(0, …)`; **`letterLSB()` does not**, so ink starting left of the origin — trivial in Draw mode, where the canvas is far wider than the glyph box — fed a negative width into the left margin rect. Per the SVG spec a negative width is an error and the element is **not rendered**, so the band silently disappeared *and* Chromium logged `<rect> attribute width: A negative value is not valid` on **every** stage render (draw, trace, align and anchors all draw it). Isolated with an `innerHTML` setter wrapper that captured 4 real assignments with JS stacks — the console shows only 1 because Chromium collapses the repeats, so the raw console count understates this class. Clamped **at the render site, not in the accessor**, so the metrics readout keeps reporting the true ink-left; `wideFormGuidesSVG` carries the identical `marginZone(0, …)` shape and got the same guard. Verified in EN/light/1280 and HE/dark/800: with the repro condition still present (ink-left −266, derived LSB −311) all four work modes render **0** negative rects and **0** SVG errors, and a **negative control** that slides the ink inside the advance still draws both bands (34.9 and 12.8 units).

The overlap loop now skips a pair **only when both contours are outer**; **outer/hole and hole/hole overlaps — the cases that actually cancel a region — still fire** (hole/hole was added deliberately: two overlapping counters double-subtract and invert, so "windings differ" alone would have been a narrower rule than correctness allows). A permanent unclearable warning is how a teacher learns to **ignore the panel that also carries the real findings about her own letters**. | verified headless light+dark × 1280/800, pageerror==0, 44 checks: untouched project raises **0** outline warnings and the QA panel keeps its placement verdict; **positive controls** confirm outer+hole and hole+hole still flag, self-intersection still detected (its call signature changed), normal counters + disjoint contours still clear, and a contour with **no** `winding` field still defaults to outer.
